"""Lane M, M8: surface-history metrics by hand."""

from __future__ import annotations

import math

import pandas as pd
import pytest

from ohcamel_quant.models.surface_metrics import day_metrics, interp_linear, interp_total_variance, vrp

TERM = pd.DataFrame({"T": [0.05, 0.15], "atm_iv": [0.20, 0.30], "rr_25d": [-0.02, -0.04], "bf_25d": [0.01, 0.02],
                     "mf_var": [0.05, 0.10]})


def test_atm_interpolates_in_total_variance():
    # w1 = 0.2^2 x 0.05 = 0.002, w2 = 0.3^2 x 0.15 = 0.0135; T = 30/365 = 0.0821918;
    # w = 0.002 + 0.0115 x (0.0821918 - 0.05)/0.1 = 0.00570205; iv = sqrt(w/T) = 0.2633913.
    assert interp_total_variance(TERM, "atm_iv", 30 / 365) == pytest.approx(0.26339134382131846, rel=1e-12)


def test_no_extrapolation():
    assert interp_total_variance(TERM, "atm_iv", 0.5) is None and interp_linear(TERM, "rr_25d", 0.01) is None


def test_rr_bf_interpolate_linearly_in_T():
    x = (30 / 365 - 0.05) / 0.10
    assert interp_linear(TERM, "rr_25d", 30 / 365) == pytest.approx(-0.02 + x * (-0.02))


def test_day_metrics_and_vrp():
    m = day_metrics(TERM)
    assert m["atm_iv_30d"] == pytest.approx(0.26339134382131846) and m["atm_iv_90d"] is None  # 90d > 0.15: no extrap
    assert m["term_slope"] is None and m["n_slices"] == 2
    assert m["mf_var_30d"] == pytest.approx((0.05 * 0.05 * (0.15 - 30 / 365) + 0.15 * 0.10 * (30 / 365 - 0.05))
                                            / 0.10 / (30 / 365))  # Cboe total-variance interpolation
    assert vrp(0.04, 1e-4) == pytest.approx(0.04 - 252e-4)
    assert math.isnan(vrp(float("nan"), 1e-4))

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** `models/surface_metrics.py`:

```python
"""P7: one row per underlying per day from a fitted surface's term table (compute plan M8).

ATM IV interpolates in total variance w = sigma^2 T (calendar-arbitrage-free between
slices); RR/BF interpolate linearly in T; neither extrapolates (None outside the
quoted terms). The 30-day model-free variance is Cboe's constant-maturity
interpolation (options.metrics.constant_maturity_variance), refused when it would
extrapolate. VRP = 30-day implied variance - 252 x the HAR 22-day daily-variance forecast.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from collections.abc import Callable

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from ..options.metrics import constant_maturity_variance

TARGETS = {"30d": 30.0 / 365.0, "90d": 90.0 / 365.0}


def _bracket(term: pd.DataFrame, col: str, T: float) -> tuple[pd.Series, pd.Series] | None:
    t = term[["T", col]].dropna().sort_values("T")
    lo, hi = t[t["T"] <= T], t[t["T"] >= T]
    if lo.empty or hi.empty:
        return None
    return lo.iloc[-1], hi.iloc[0]


def interp_total_variance(term: pd.DataFrame, col: str, T: float) -> float | None:
    b = _bracket(term, col, T)
    if b is None:
        return None
    (t1, v1), (t2, v2) = (b[0]["T"], b[0][col]), (b[1]["T"], b[1][col])
    if t2 == t1:
        return float(v1)
    w = v1 * v1 * t1 + (v2 * v2 * t2 - v1 * v1 * t1) * (T - t1) / (t2 - t1)
    return float(math.sqrt(w / T)) if w > 0 else None


def interp_linear(term: pd.DataFrame, col: str, T: float) -> float | None:
    b = _bracket(term, col, T)
    if b is None:
        return None
    (t1, v1), (t2, v2) = (b[0]["T"], b[0][col]), (b[1]["T"], b[1][col])
    return float(v1) if t2 == t1 else float(v1 + (v2 - v1) * (T - t1) / (t2 - t1))


def day_metrics(term: pd.DataFrame) -> dict[str, float | None]:
    a30 = interp_total_variance(term, "atm_iv", TARGETS["30d"])
    a90 = interp_total_variance(term, "atm_iv", TARGETS["90d"])
    mf = None
    if "mf_var" in term:
        try:
            cm = constant_maturity_variance(list(zip(term["T"], term["mf_var"], strict=True)), TARGETS["30d"])
            mf = None if cm.get("extrapolated") else float(cm["sigma2"])
        except ValueError:
            mf = None
    return {"atm_iv_30d": a30, "atm_iv_90d": a90, "term_slope": None if a30 is None or a90 is None else a90 - a30,
            "rr25_30d": interp_linear(term, "rr_25d", TARGETS["30d"]) if "rr_25d" in term else None,
            "bf25_30d": interp_linear(term, "bf_25d", TARGETS["30d"]) if "bf_25d" in term else None,
            "mf_var_30d": mf, "n_slices": int(len(term))}


def vrp(mf_var_30d: float | None, har_22d_daily: float | None) -> float:
    if mf_var_30d is None or har_22d_daily is None:
        return float("nan")
    return float(mf_var_30d) - 252.0 * float(har_22d_daily)


def rate_curve(row: pd.Series) -> Callable[[float], float]:
    """FRED CMT yields (percent, bond-equivalent) -> continuous ``r = 2 ln(1 + y/200)``, linear in tenor
    (the options router's convention), from one dated row of ``MarketData.treasury_curve``."""
    last = row.dropna()
    if len(last) < 2:
        raise ValueError("fewer than two tenors on the Treasury curve")
    tenors = np.asarray(last.index, dtype=float)
    rates = 2.0 * np.log1p(last.to_numpy(dtype=float) / 200.0)
    o = np.argsort(tenors)
    return lambda T: float(np.interp(T, tenors[o], rates[o]))
```

```python
"""Lane M, M8: vol.surface_history over a temp warehouse of BSM-priced quotes (hand-built to check the
pipeline against a known smile, as tests/test_options_svi_parity.py does; nothing is published)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from datetime import date, datetime

import duckdb
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import pytest

from lane_m_harness import publish_artifact
from ohcamel_quant.jobs.context import JobContext
from ohcamel_quant.options import bsm
from ohcamel_quant.products import surface_history
from ohcamel_quant.products.io import read_table
from ohcamel_quant.warehouse.db import open_rw
from ohcamel_quant.warehouse.ingest.base import insert_frame, log_row
from ohcamel_quant.warehouse.ingest.options import INSERT

SPOT, R, Q = 100.0, 0.04, 0.01
ASOF = pd.Timestamp("2026-06-01 16:15:00")


def _quotes():
    rows = []
    for dte in (30, 60, 120):
        expiry = (ASOF + pd.Timedelta(days=dte)).normalize()
        T = (expiry + pd.Timedelta(hours=16) - ASOF).total_seconds() / (365.0 * 86400)
        F = SPOT * np.exp((R - Q) * T)
        for K in np.arange(70.0, 131.0, 2.5):
            k = np.log(K / F)
            iv = 0.20 - 0.10 * k + 0.30 * k * k            # ATM 0.20 at every expiry
            for cp in ("C", "P"):
                m = float(bsm.bsm_price(SPOT, K, T, iv, R, Q, cp))
                rows.append({"underlying": "SPY", "asof": ASOF.normalize(), "expiry": expiry, "strike": K, "cp": cp,
                             "bid": max(m - 0.02, 0.01), "ask": m + 0.02, "last": m, "volume": 10.0,
                             "open_interest": 100.0, "source": "test"})
    return pd.DataFrame(rows)


@pytest.fixture
def wh(tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path / "w.duckdb"
    with open_rw(path) as con:
        insert_frame(con, INSERT, _quotes())
        log_row(con, "option_snapshots", "SPY", "ok", 1, json.dumps({"spot": SPOT, "as_of": ASOF.isoformat()}),
                date(2026, 6, 1), ran_at=datetime(2026, 6, 1, 20, 20))
    return path


def ctx(market, wh):
    return JobContext(job_id="t", params={}, threads=1, progress_fn=lambda f, m: None, cancelled_fn=lambda: False,
                      _market=market, _warehouse=duckdb.connect(str(wh), read_only=True))


def test_one_day_of_history_from_a_known_smile(market, wh, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    db, root = tmp_path / "jobs.sqlite", tmp_path / "artifacts"
    monkeypatch.setattr(surface_history, "DB_PATH", db)
    publish_artifact(db, root, "vol.forecast_league",
                     {"forecasts": pd.DataFrame({"ticker": ["SPY"], "asof": ["2026-05-29"], "har_22d": [1e-4]})})
    spec = surface_history.run({}, ctx(market, wh))
    assert spec.verdict == "DESCRIPTIVE ONLY" and "1 day" in spec.verdict_detail
    h = spec.tables["history"].iloc[0]
    assert h["underlying"] == "SPY" and h["asof"] == "2026-06-01"
    assert h["atm_iv_30d"] == pytest.approx(0.20, abs=0.005)   # the constructed smile's ATM level
    assert abs(h["term_slope"]) < 0.01 and h["rr25_30d"] < 0         # flat ATM term; put skew from -0.10 k
    assert h["mf_var_30d"] > 0 and h["vrp"] == pytest.approx(h["mf_var_30d"] - 252e-4)
    assert spec.tables["summary"].set_index("underlying").loc["SPY", "days"] == 1


def test_a_second_run_only_adds_new_days(market, wh, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    db, root = tmp_path / "jobs.sqlite", tmp_path / "artifacts"
    monkeypatch.setattr(surface_history, "DB_PATH", db)
    first = surface_history.run({}, ctx(market, wh))
    _, path = publish_artifact(db, root, "vol.surface_history", first.tables, verdict=first.verdict)
    second = surface_history.run({}, ctx(market, wh))
    pd.testing.assert_frame_equal(second.tables["history"], read_table(path, "history"))  # nothing new: carried


def test_surface_history_with_no_snapshots_is_insufficient_data(market, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(surface_history, "DB_PATH", tmp_path / "jobs.sqlite")
    path = tmp_path / "empty.duckdb"
    with open_rw(path):
        pass
    spec = surface_history.run({}, ctx(market, path))
    assert spec.verdict == "INSUFFICIENT DATA" and "0 days of history" in spec.verdict_detail
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Implement** `products/surface_history.py`:

```python
"""P7 job: ``vol.surface_history`` (nightly, heavy, class M): each unprocessed (underlying, asof) snapshot
-> chain -> SVI surface -> one history row. The history is carried forward from the previous artifact and
only grows; the snapshots themselves cannot be re-fetched (Lane C C6)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from datetime import timedelta
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import pandas as pd

from ..data.base import DataUnavailable
from ..data.market import OptionChain
from ..jobs.artifacts import ArtifactSpec
from ..models.surface_metrics import day_metrics, rate_curve, vrp
from ..options.chain import analyze_chain
from ..options.surface import build_surface

KIND = "vol.surface_history"
DB_PATH: Path | None = None
COLS = ["underlying", "asof", "atm_iv_30d", "atm_iv_90d", "term_slope", "rr25_30d", "bf25_30d", "mf_var_30d",
        "vrp", "vrp_note", "n_slices", "spot"]


def _pending(con: Any, done: set[tuple[str, str]]) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
    rows = con.execute('SELECT DISTINCT underlying, CAST("asof" AS VARCHAR) FROM option_snapshots '
                       'ORDER BY 2, 1').fetchall()
    return [(u, a) for u, a in rows if (u, a) not in done]


def _chain(con: Any, und: str, asof: str) -> OptionChain:
    q = con.execute('SELECT expiry, strike, cp, bid, ask, last, volume, open_interest FROM option_snapshots '
                    'WHERE underlying = ? AND "asof" = ?', [und, asof]).df()
    log = con.execute("SELECT detail FROM ingest_log WHERE dataset = 'option_snapshots' AND key = ? "
                      "AND data_asof = ? AND status = 'ok' ORDER BY ran_at DESC LIMIT 1", [und, asof]).fetchone()
    if log is None:
        raise ValueError(f"{und} {asof}: no ingest_log row with the snapshot's spot")
    d = json.loads(log[0])
    quotes = q.rename(columns={"cp": "type"}).assign(expiry=lambda x: pd.to_datetime(x["expiry"]), root=und)
    return OptionChain(underlying=und, spot=float(d["spot"]), as_of=pd.Timestamp(d["as_of"]).tz_localize(None),
                       quotes=quotes)


def _har(prev_league: tuple[dict, Path] | None) -> pd.DataFrame | None:
    from .io import read_table

    return read_table(prev_league[1], "forecasts") if prev_league else None


def run(params: dict[str, Any], ctx: Any) -> ArtifactSpec:
    from .io import previous_artifact, read_table

    prev = previous_artifact(KIND, db_path=DB_PATH)
    hist = read_table(prev[1], "history") if prev else None
    hist = hist if hist is not None else pd.DataFrame(columns=COLS)
    done = set(zip(hist["underlying"], hist["asof"], strict=True))
    try:
        todo = _pending(ctx.warehouse, done)
    except DataUnavailable as e:
        todo = []
        notes = [f"warehouse unavailable: {e}"]
    else:
        notes = []
    har = _har(previous_artifact("vol.forecast_league", db_path=DB_PATH))
    rows, errors = [], []
    for i, (und, asof) in enumerate(todo):
        ctx.check_cancelled()
        try:
            chain = _chain(ctx.warehouse, und, asof)
            try:
                tc = ctx.market.treasury_curve(pd.Timestamp(asof).date() - timedelta(days=30), pd.Timestamp(asof).date())
                curve = rate_curve(tc.data.dropna(how="all").iloc[-1])
            except (DataUnavailable, ValueError, IndexError):
                curve = None
            surf = build_surface(analyze_chain(chain, rate_curve=curve), use_kernels=True)
            m = day_metrics(surf.term.reset_index())
        except (DataUnavailable, ValueError) as e:
            errors.append({"underlying": und, "asof": asof, "error": str(e)})
            continue
        h = None if har is None else har[(har["ticker"] == und) & (har["asof"] <= asof)]
        if h is None or h.empty or "har_22d" not in h or pd.isna(h["har_22d"].iloc[-1]):
            v, why = float("nan"), "no HAR forecast dated on or before the snapshot (minute bars cover ~50 names)"
        else:
            v, why = vrp(m["mf_var_30d"], float(h["har_22d"].iloc[-1])), None
        rows.append({"underlying": und, "asof": asof, **m, "vrp": v, "vrp_note": why, "spot": chain.spot})
        ctx.progress((i + 1) / max(len(todo), 1), f"{und} {asof}")
    if rows:
        new = pd.DataFrame(rows, columns=COLS)
        hist = new if hist.empty else pd.concat([hist, new], ignore_index=True)
    days = hist["asof"].nunique()
    if hist.empty:
        return ArtifactSpec(tables={"history": hist, "errors": pd.DataFrame(errors)}, data_asof=None,
                            provenance=[], notes=notes, verdict="INSUFFICIENT DATA",
                            verdict_detail="0 days of history: no option snapshots yet")
    summary = (hist.groupby("underlying").agg(days=("asof", "nunique"), first=("asof", "min"), last=("asof", "max"))
               .reset_index())
    return ArtifactSpec(
        tables={"history": hist, "summary": summary, "errors": pd.DataFrame(errors, columns=["underlying", "asof", "error"])},
        data_asof=str(hist["asof"].max()), provenance=[{"source": "warehouse:option_snapshots (Cboe delayed)"}],
        notes=[*notes, "SVI per expiry; ATM in total variance, RR/BF linear in T, no extrapolation; "
                       "model-free variance by the Cboe method"],
        verdict="DESCRIPTIVE ONLY",
        verdict_detail=f"{days} day{'s' if days != 1 else ''} of history across {hist['underlying'].nunique()} "
                       "underlyings; a measurement, not a strategy")
```

`surf.term` is indexed by slice, with columns `T`, `atm_iv`, `rr_25d`, `bf_25d` and `mf_var` (see `options/surface.build_surface`). `reset_index()` makes it a plain frame for `day_metrics`.

## Whole-lane review and Gate GM

- [ ] **Whole-lane review.** One fresh reviewer reads the lane diff (`git diff ship/main...ship/lane-m`) against this plan, the ship spec §6, the compute plan's Lane M and contracts II.2–II.7, both pre-registrations and the charter. The reviewer checks specifically:
  - Every Review Focus test exists and fails if its rule is broken. Run three mutations by hand:
    - `holdout_decision` always returning `"evaluate"`;
    - `purge` dropping the embargo;
    - `farm_dsr` using the cell's trial count.
  - No interpretation point drifted from `config.yaml`.
  - No handler writes outside its artifact.
  - `models/` imports no I/O.

  Fix wave, then re-run the full suite.
- [ ] **Local gate (this lane can prove it):**
  - `cd quant && .venv/bin/pytest -q` green.
  - `.venv/bin/ruff check src tests` clean.
  - `scripts/check-counts.sh` exits 0.
  - `cd quant/web && npm run typecheck && npm test` green.
  - With the Rust wheel (`make kernels-dev`), the M2/M3/M5 tests are green again on `OHCAMEL_QUANT_KERNELS=rust` and `=python`.
  - `python -c "from ohcamel_quant.jobs.schedules import load_schedules; load_schedules()"` succeeds.
  - Every Lane M kind appears in `LANE_M_KINDS` and every product in `ENTRIES`.
- [ ] **Gate GM (measured on the droplet in Phase 3's soak, ship plan; not by this lane):**
  - Each product has run for five consecutive sessions, with artifacts present and `data_asof` current.
  - Each kind's observed `peak_rss_bytes` is under its class budget. If not, raise the class in `kinds.py` and `schedules.yaml` together.
  - G-RT passes during a nightly batch.
  - The `hostd` history shows the batch slice near its cap through the batch window and idle after, with a screenshot committed.
  - EXP-Q01's universe is frozen (M6 Step 15) and I-Q01-7's dollar-volume sign is confirmed by the owner before its first droplet run.

## Handoffs to other lanes

- **Lane P2 (pages)** reads `GET /api/artifacts/{kind}/latest`, where a 404 means `NOT YET RUN`, and the tables below. Every payload's first key is `verdict`; render it with `Verdict`. Each product also has `stale` and `manifest.data_asof`.

  | Page | Kind | Tables |
  |---|---|---|
  | Risk · Atlas | `risk.mc_atlas` | `summary`, `euler`, `members` |
  | Deck / Risk intraday | `risk.mc_intraday` | `summary`; also `mc_var` in `/api/deck/reading` |
  | Volatility · Forecasts | `vol.forecast_league` | `league`, `dm`, `summary` |
  | Portfolio · Covariance league | `cov.league` | `league`, `tests` |
  | Research · Farm | `farm.sweep` | `leaderboard` (verdict first, FAIL count largest), `cells` |
  | Research · Models | `models.xs_lgbm` | `gates`, `holdout`, `holdout_returns`, `holdout_costs`, `holdout_regimes`, `holdout_ic`, `holdout_deciles`, `scores` |
  | Rates & Macro · Regimes | `regime.hmm` | `probs` (`p_high` filtered; `p_high_smoothed_history` labelled history), `holdout`, `gates` |
  | Volatility · Surface history | `vol.surface_history` | `history`, `summary` ("n days of history") |
  | Front page | all of the above | `.../latest` heads only |

  Methodology entries `p1-mc-atlas` … `p7-surface-history` are in `models.ts`. REFS keys not yet in `references.ts` are cited in text: Corsi 2009, Diebold–Mariano 1995, Hansen–Lunde–Nason 2011, Patton 2011, Nelson 1991, Asness–Moskowitz–Pedersen 2013, Ke et al. 2017, López de Prado 2018, Ang–Bekaert 2002.
- **Lane P2, alignment with what `ship/lane-p2` already reads (checked 2026-10-07).**
  - The kind names follow P2's `lib/artifacts.ts` `KINDS`: `farm.sweep`, `models.xs_lgbm` (EXP-Q01) and `regime.hmm` (EXP-Q02).
  - Table reads return the II.3 frame keys at the top level, so `frameRecords` works unchanged.
  - Atlas `summary`/`euler` and the cov `league` columns match P2's readers. `euler` carries `pct_es`, `method` and `horizon`; `league` carries `realized_vol` and `lw_p`.
  - P2 still has to make four changes:
    - add `"DESCRIPTIVE ONLY"` to `VerdictWord` (P1, P2, P3, P7 and a non-passing EXP-Q02 use it);
    - flip `PREREGISTERED` to `true` for both experiments (approved 2026-10-06; an unfrozen Q01 universe reads as `INSUFFICIENT DATA` from the API, not as awaiting approval);
    - filter cov `league` rows by `universe`;
    - prefer the API's `stale` flag (schedules.yaml `max_age`) over its own 36 h constant.
- **Lane F (Deck).** The reading's top-level `mc_var` is `{var_usd, es_usd, alpha, horizon_days: 1, method: "fhs", paths: null, as_of, finished_at, stale, book, artifact_id}`, or `null`. That matches `mcVarOf` on `ship/lane-f`, which ignores the extra keys. `as_of` is the live mark's time, falling back to `finished_at`.
- **Lane H.**
  - The `quant/Dockerfile` changes (libgomp1, `COPY research/experiments/`, `import lightgbm`) need CI's image build.
  - The EXP-Q01 freeze (M6 Step 15) runs after the backfill.
  - Gate GM is measured in the soak.
  - Security: artifact routes are already traversal-safe (Review Focus 4); include them in H4's path-traversal test.
- **Owner.**
  - Confirm I-Q01-7's dollar-volume sign (−1).
  - Approve the universe freeze commit.
  - Read the first holdout results. Under the charter the kill decision is the owner's; under the pre-registrations every model stays advisory whatever its verdict.

## Self-review (done while writing)

- **Spec coverage.**
  - Ship Lane M brief: verdict in the manifest and returned first (M1); M6 and M7 exactly per the pre-registrations (M6 and M7, with interpretation points made explicit); GM measured in the soak (Gate GM).
  - Compute plan M1–M8: M1 feature store, leakage guards and purged k-fold (plus the artifact API every product needs); M2 atlas, including the deck's `mc_var` (Lane F's wire shape); M3 GARCH/GJR/EGARCH/HAR/EWMA with QLIKE, MSE, DM and MCS; M4 estimators, PCA factor and a Ledoit–Wolf-style HAC test; M5 farm with grids up to 256, CSCV S=16, SPA, DSR, costs and verdict first; M6 LightGBM with purged walk-forward, IC/rank-IC HAC, deciles, DSR, PBO and a model card; M7 own-EM HMM tested on a hand-built series, BIC 2–4, filtered and smoothed; M8 SVI on C6 snapshots, ATM, RR/BF, term slope, model-free variance, VRP vs HAR and "n days".
  - Methodology entries for all seven. Kinds and schedules for all eight kinds, with heavy kinds outside the session.
- **Deviations from the compute plan, with reasons:**
  - P5 runs on the ETF universe: the charter and option A of the approved pre-registration.
  - P5 runs monthly and P6 weekly: the pre-registrations' frequencies, and Global Constraint 3.
  - The value and quality features from `sec_facts` are not built: EXP-Q01's approved feature list has none, and single names are out of scope. `asof_join` keeps the point-in-time rule available for when a survivorship-free source arrives.
  - The pre-registration fixes the gates as the charter's, which replace the spec's earlier DSR > 0.95.
- **Placeholder scan.** Every step carries its code or exact command. The two hand-offs that cannot run here (the universe freeze, Gate GM) are named with their exact procedure.
- **Type consistency.**
  - `ArtifactSpec.verdict` and `verdict_detail` (M1) are used by every handler.
  - `charter_verdict(..., extra=...)` is used by M5 and M6.
  - `previous_artifact`, `read_table` and `latest_with_path` (M1) are used in M2, M5, M6, M7 and M8.
  - `walk_forward`, `owned_months` and `purge` (M1) are used in M6.
  - `qlike` (M3) is used in M7.
  - Every product's HAR forecast feeds M8 through the `forecasts` table and its `har_22d` column (M3).
  - Each module's `DB_PATH` hook is consistent across M2 (`atlas.DEFAULT_DB`), M5, M6, M7 and M8.
- **Review Focus.** Each of the five lines names its pinning tests in the owning task.
- **Dry run (2026-10-07).** Every Python code block in M1–M8 was extracted into a scratch copy of `ship/main` at 49a5c69, with the M1 contract edits, kinds, schedules and Methodology entries applied, and run offline: the NumPy kernel engine and LightGBM 4.7. Results:
  - All 139 tests in the Lane M files plus the touched jobs, deck and options suites pass. The only failure was a deck test that needs `quant/web`, which the scratch copy lacked.
  - `ruff` is clean after `--fix`, which only re-wraps imports.
  - The seven `models.ts` entries type-check under `tsc --strict`.

  Not dry-run: the Dockerfile edit, the `uv.lock` change, the web `npm test`, and the Rust-engine runs. Those are verified in their own steps.
- **Fix round 1 (2026-10-07), after review.** Three blocking issues were fixed. The new tests were seen failing against the previous plan code, then passing:
  - **Q01 boundary leak.** Windows are now keyed by label entry (`window_keys`, I-Q01-1). Before the fix, the old selection's stitched returns ran through 2023-01-31 in the shortened test config. Now selection ends at the last 2022 session and the holdout starts at the first 2023 session.
  - **Q02 boundary leak.** Selection keeps a week only if `rv_end ≤ selection_end`. Weeks whose target straddles the boundary are purged and counted (`boundary_weeks_purged`).
  - **Universe freeze.** Adjustment is now judged by source, never by `adj_close == close` (I-Q01-9). GLD-like funds are kept. A Stooq segment stitched to a vendor is excluded. `coverage_from_warehouse` is tested on a real migrated DuckDB.

  Re-run of the dry-run copy: 146 passed, with the same single `quant/web` deck failure as before.
